3083: function renderQuests(){const filter=game.contentView.questFilter||'active',all=Object.values(QUESTS).filter(q=>filter==='main'?q.type==='main':filter==='side'?q.type==='side':filter==='completed'?game.quests.completed.includes(q.id):game.quests.active.includes(q.id)||(!q.requires||game.quests.claimed.includes(q.requires))&&!game.quests.completed.includes(q.id)).slice(0,35),normals=all.map(q=>questCard(q,game.quests.progress?.[q.id]||0,game.quests.completed.includes(q.id),game.quests.claimed.includes(q.id))).join(''),dailies=game.quests.daily.map(q=>questCard(q,q.progress||0,q.complete,q.claimed,true)).join('');return `${screenHead('10 CAPÍTULOS','Misiones',`${Object.values(QUESTS).filter(q=>q.type==='main').length} PRINCIPALES`)}<select class="content-filter" data-content-filter="questFilter"><option value="active" ${filter==='active'?'selected':''}>Disponibles y activas</option><option value="main" ${filter==='main'?'selected':''}>Historia principal</option><option value="side" ${filter==='side'?'selected':''}>Secundarias</option><option value="completed" ${filter==='completed'?'selected':''}>Completadas</option></select><h3 class="section-title">DIARIAS · ${game.quests.dailyDate}</h3>${dailies}<h3 class="section-title">AVENTURAS · ${all.length} MOSTRADAS</h3>${normals||'<div class="empty-state">No hay misiones en este filtro.</div>'}`;}

3093: function renderDungeonsV9(){const active=game.activities.dungeon,rotation=localAffixRotation(),family=id=>FAMILY_TRAITS[ENEMIES[id]?.family]?.name||'Mixta';return `${screenHead('LLAVES DE DESAFÍO','Mazmorras V15',`⚡ ${game.energy.current}`)}<div class="modifier-banner"><b>${rotation.map(a=>a.name).join(' · ')}</b><span>Rotación local semanal · niveles 4/7/10 añaden afijos</span></div>${DUNGEONS.map(d=>{const locked=game.player.level<d.level||(d.prestige||0)>(game.player.prestige?.level||0),run=active?.id===d.id,otherRun=Boolean(active&&!run),max=game.challengeKeys.levels[d.id]||0,selected=Math.min(max,game.challengeKeys.selected[d.id]??max),boss=ENEMIES[d.rooms.at(-1)],wish=game.wishlist.some(id=>UNIQUE_ITEMS[id]?.setId===d.lootSet||id===d.lootSet);return `<article class="dungeon-card-v9 ${wish?'wish-source':''}"><div class="dungeon-main"><i>${d.icon}</i><div><small>${family(d.rooms[0])} · ${d.rooms.length} ENCUENTROS · JEFE ${boss?.name}</small><h3>${d.name}${selected?` +${selected}`:''}</h3><p>Mecánica: ${d.id.includes('fire')||d.id.includes('blood')?'controlar Quemaduras y ataques cargados':d.id.includes('mine')||d.id.includes('titan')?'romper estabilidad de constructos':'gestionar estados y élites'}.</p><em>${d.lootSet?`BOTÍN · ${SETS[d.lootSet]?.name||d.lootSet}`:'BOTÍN ESPECÍFICO · materiales y recetas'}${wish?' · ★ CONTIENE UN OBJETIVO':''}</em></div></div><div class="key-row"><button data-key="${d.id}:${Math.max(0,selected-1)}" ${selected<=0||otherRun?'disabled':''}>−</button><span>${otherRun?'OTRA MAZMORRA EN CURSO':`LLAVE ${selected?`+${selected}`:'BASE'} · MÁX ${max||'—'}`}</span><button data-key="${d.id}:${Math.min(max,selected+1)}" ${selected>=max||otherRun?'disabled':''}>+</button><button data-dungeon="${d.id}" ${locked||otherRun?'disabled':''}>${run?`SALA ${active.room+1}`:otherRun?'EN CURSO':'ENTRAR'}</button></div></article>`;}).join('')}`;}

3121: function startFightV9(mi,ni,{skipEvent=false,forceElite=false,forceRare=false,eventChoice=null}={}){if(!campaignNodeAvailable(mi,ni)){toast('Zona o encuentro bloqueado');return;}const map=MAPS[mi],base=ENEMIES[map.nodes[ni]];if(!skipEvent&&!base.boss&&Math.random()<.18){const event=ZONE_EVENTS[Math.floor(Math.random()*ZONE_EVENTS.length)];game.mapEvent={v9:true,eventId:event.id,zoneId:map.id,pending:true,pendingFight:{mi,ni},expiresAt:Date.now()+30*60*1000};saveGame(game);render();return;}let enemy=createEncounter(base.id,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite});const nemesis=game.nemesis;if(nemesis&&nemesis.zone===map.id&&!base.boss&&Math.random()<.14){enemy=createEncounter(nemesis.baseId,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite:true});enemy.name=nemesis.name;enemy.level=Math.max(enemy.level,nemesis.level);enemy.nemesis=true;enemy.eliteAffixes=[...nemesis.affixes];enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*(1+nemesis.victories*.12));enemy.attack=Math.round(enemy.attack*(1+nemesis.victories*.08));enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;}else if(!base.boss&&(forceRare||Math.random()<.045)){const title=RARE_TITLES[Math.floor(Math.random()*RARE_TITLES.length)];enemy=createEncounter(base.id,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite:true});enemy.name=`${base.name} ${title}`;enemy.rare=true;enemy.elite=true;enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*1.18);enemy.gold=Math.round(enemy.gold*2);enemy.xp=Math.round(enemy.xp*1.7);enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;game.rareCodex[`${base.id}:${title}`]={name:enemy.name,family:enemy.family,zone:enemy.zone,affixes:[...(enemy.eliteAffixes||[])],seenAt:Date.now()};game.notifications.push({type:'rare',text:`ENCUENTRO RARO · ${enemy.name}`,at:Date.now()});}if(eventChoice){enemy.xp=Math.round(enemy.xp*1.2);enemy.gold=Math.round(enemy.gold*1.3);}combat=new CombatSession(game,enemy,`${mi}-${ni}`,{activity:{type:'campaign',mapEvent:eventChoice||null}});combat.mapIndex=mi;combat.nodeIndex=ni;combatReward=null;saveGame(game);render();}

3123: function startDungeonFightV9(){const run=game.activities.dungeon,d=DUNGEONS.find(x=>x.id===run?.id);if(!d||!Number.isInteger(run.room)||run.room<0||run.room>=d.rooms.length){game.activities.dungeon=null;saveGame(game);toast('La expedición de mazmorra dañada se cerró con seguridad');render();return;}const level=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Number(run.keyLevel)||0)),rotation=localAffixRotation(),affixCount=level>=10?3:level>=7?2:level>=4?1:0,keyAffixes=rotation.slice(0,affixCount).map(a=>a.id),enemy=createEncounter(d.rooms[run.room],{difficulty:level>=7?'nightmare':level>=3?'veteran':game.difficulty,forceElite:run.room===2||level>=5});const scale=1+level*.13;enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*scale);enemy.attack=Math.round(enemy.attack*(1+level*.075));enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;if(keyAffixes.includes('fortified')&&(enemy.elite||enemy.boss))enemy.defense=Math.round(enemy.defense*1.35);if(keyAffixes.includes('arcaneStorm'))enemy.skills.push({name:'Tormenta de la Llave',chance:.4,multiplier:1.4,statusEffect:'curse',duration:2,interruptible:true});combat=new CombatSession(game,enemy,`dungeon-${run.runId}-${run.room}`,{activity:{type:'dungeon',id:d.id,modifier:Object.values(DUNGEON_MODIFIERS)[new Date().getUTCDate()%Object.keys(DUNGEON_MODIFIERS).length].id,keyLevel:level,keyAffixes}});combatReward=null;render();}

3127: function resolveCombatAction(action){if(!combat||combat.outcome)return;const valid=action==='attack'?combat.basicAttack():action==='defend'?combat.defend():action==='potion'?combat.usePotion('hp'):action==='cleanse'?combat.usePotion('cleanse'):combat.usePotion('mana');if(valid){AudioSystem.trigger(action==='attack'?'attack':action==='defend'?'click':'potion');triggerHaptic(action==='defend'?'defend':'tap');finishAction();}}

3093: function renderDungeonsV9(){const active=game.activities.dungeon,rotation=localAffixRotation(),family=id=>FAMILY_TRAITS[ENEMIES[id]?.family]?.name||'Mixta';return `${screenHead('LLAVES DE DESAFÍO','Mazmorras V15',`⚡ ${game.energy.current}`)}<div class="modifier-banner"><b>${rotation.map(a=>a.name).join(' · ')}</b><span>Rotación local semanal · niveles 4/7/10 añaden afijos</span></div>${DUNGEONS.map(d=>{const locked=game.player.level<d.level||(d.prestige||0)>(game.player.prestige?.level||0),run=active?.id===d.id,otherRun=Boolean(active&&!run),max=game.challengeKeys.levels[d.id]||0,selected=Math.min(max,game.challengeKeys.selected[d.id]??max),boss=ENEMIES[d.rooms.at(-1)],wish=game.wishlist.some(id=>UNIQUE_ITEMS[id]?.setId===d.lootSet||id===d.lootSet);return `<article class="dungeon-card-v9 ${wish?'wish-source':''}"><div class="dungeon-main"><i>${d.icon}</i><div><small>${family(d.rooms[0])} · ${d.rooms.length} ENCUENTROS · JEFE ${boss?.name}</small><h3>${d.name}${selected?` +${selected}`:''}</h3><p>Mecánica: ${d.id.includes('fire')||d.id.includes('blood')?'controlar Quemaduras y ataques cargados':d.id.includes('mine')||d.id.includes('titan')?'romper estabilidad de constructos':'gestionar estados y élites'}.</p><em>${d.lootSet?`BOTÍN · ${SETS[d.lootSet]?.name||d.lootSet}`:'BOTÍN ESPECÍFICO · materiales y recetas'}${wish?' · ★ CONTIENE UN OBJETIVO':''}</em></div></div><div class="key-row"><button data-key="${d.id}:${Math.max(0,selected-1)}" ${selected<=0||otherRun?'disabled':''}>−</button><span>${otherRun?'OTRA MAZMORRA EN CURSO':`LLAVE ${selected?`+${selected}`:'BASE'} · MÁX ${max||'—'}`}</span><button data-key="${d.id}:${Math.min(max,selected+1)}" ${selected>=max||otherRun?'disabled':''}>+</button><button data-dungeon="${d.id}" ${locked||otherRun?'disabled':''}>${run?`SALA ${active.room+1}`:otherRun?'EN CURSO':'ENTRAR'}</button></div></article>`;}).join('')}`;}

3121: function startFightV9(mi,ni,{skipEvent=false,forceElite=false,forceRare=false,eventChoice=null}={}){if(!campaignNodeAvailable(mi,ni)){toast('Zona o encuentro bloqueado');return;}const map=MAPS[mi],base=ENEMIES[map.nodes[ni]];if(!skipEvent&&!base.boss&&Math.random()<.18){const event=ZONE_EVENTS[Math.floor(Math.random()*ZONE_EVENTS.length)];game.mapEvent={v9:true,eventId:event.id,zoneId:map.id,pending:true,pendingFight:{mi,ni},expiresAt:Date.now()+30*60*1000};saveGame(game);render();return;}let enemy=createEncounter(base.id,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite});const nemesis=game.nemesis;if(nemesis&&nemesis.zone===map.id&&!base.boss&&Math.random()<.14){enemy=createEncounter(nemesis.baseId,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite:true});enemy.name=nemesis.name;enemy.level=Math.max(enemy.level,nemesis.level);enemy.nemesis=true;enemy.eliteAffixes=[...nemesis.affixes];enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*(1+nemesis.victories*.12));enemy.attack=Math.round(enemy.attack*(1+nemesis.victories*.08));enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;}else if(!base.boss&&(forceRare||Math.random()<.045)){const title=RARE_TITLES[Math.floor(Math.random()*RARE_TITLES.length)];enemy=createEncounter(base.id,{difficulty:game.difficulty,forceElite:true});enemy.name=`${base.name} ${title}`;enemy.rare=true;enemy.elite=true;enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*1.18);enemy.gold=Math.round(enemy.gold*2);enemy.xp=Math.round(enemy.xp*1.7);enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;game.rareCodex[`${base.id}:${title}`]={name:enemy.name,family:enemy.family,zone:enemy.zone,affixes:[...(enemy.eliteAffixes||[])],seenAt:Date.now()};game.notifications.push({type:'rare',text:`ENCUENTRO RARO · ${enemy.name}`,at:Date.now()});}if(eventChoice){enemy.xp=Math.round(enemy.xp*1.2);enemy.gold=Math.round(enemy.gold*1.3);}combat=new CombatSession(game,enemy,`${mi}-${ni}`,{activity:{type:'campaign',mapEvent:eventChoice||null}});combat.mapIndex=mi;combat.nodeIndex=ni;combatReward=null;saveGame(game);render();}

3123: function startDungeonFightV9(){const run=game.activities.dungeon,d=DUNGEONS.find(x=>x.id===run?.id);if(!d||!Number.isInteger(run.room)||run.room<0||run.room>=d.rooms.length){game.activities.dungeon=null;saveGame(game);toast('La expedición de mazmorra dañada se cerró con seguridad');render();return;}const level=Math.max(0,Math.min(10,Number(run.keyLevel)||0)),rotation=localAffixRotation(),affixCount=level>=10?3:level>=7?2:level>=4?1:0,keyAffixes=rotation.slice(0,affixCount).map(a=>a.id),enemy=createEncounter(d.rooms[run.room],{difficulty:level>=7?'nightmare':level>=3?'veteran':game.difficulty,forceElite:run.room===2||level>=5});const scale=1+level*.13;enemy.hp=Math.round(enemy.hp*scale);enemy.attack=Math.round(enemy.attack*(1+level*.075));enemy.maxHp=enemy.hp;if(keyAffixes.includes('fortified')&&(enemy.elite||enemy.boss))enemy.defense=Math.round(enemy.defense*1.35);if(keyAffixes.includes('arcaneStorm'))enemy.skills.push({name:'Tormenta de la Llave',chance:.4,multiplier:1.4,statusEffect:'curse',duration:2,interruptible:true});combat=new CombatSession(game,enemy,`dungeon-${run.runId}-${run.room}`,{activity:{type:'dungeon',id:d.id,modifier:Object.values(DUNGEON_MODIFIERS)[new Date().getUTCDate()%Object.keys(DUNGEON_MODIFIERS).length].id,keyLevel:level,keyAffixes}});combatReward=null;render();}

3083: function renderQuests(){const filter=game.contentView.questFilter||'active',all=Object.values(QUESTS).filter(q=>filter==='main'?q.type==='main':filter==='side'?q.type==='side':filter==='completed'?game.quests.completed.includes(q.id):game.quests.active.includes(q.id)||(!q.requires||game.quests.claimed.includes(q.requires))&&!game.quests.completed.includes(q.id)).slice(0,35),normals=all.map(q=>questCard(q,game.quests.progress?.[q.id]||0,game.quests.completed.includes(q.id),game.quests.claimed.includes(q.id))).join(''),dailies=game.quests.daily.map(q=>questCard(q,q.progress||0,q.complete,q.claimed,true)).join('');return `${screenHead('10 CAPÍTULOS','Misiones',`${Object.values(QUESTS).filter(q=>q.type==='main').length} PRINCIPALES`)}<select class="content-filter" data-content-filter="questFilter"><option value="active" ${filter==='active'?'selected':''}>Disponibles y activas</option><option value="main" ${filter==='main'?'selected':''}>Historia principal</option><option value="side" ${filter==='side'?'selected':''}>Secundarias</option><option value="completed" ${filter==='completed'?'selected':''}>Completadas</option></select><h3 class="section-title">DIARIAS · ${game.quests.dailyDate}</h3>${dailies}<h3 class="section-title">AVENTURAS · ${all.length} MOSTRADAS</h3>${normals||'<div class="empty-state">No hay misiones en este filtro.</div>'}`;}
